# Trading Knowledge Center ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â Claude Skill + Structured Knowledge Base

## TL;DR

> **Quick Summary**: Build a comprehensive trading knowledge encyclopedia implemented as a Claude skill (`.claude/skills/trading-knowledge/`) backed by structured markdown files. Covers market microstructure, order flow theory, auction theory, NinjaTrader strategies, DEEP6 signal system, academic research, and live market patterns. Starts by extracting the massive body of existing DEEP6 knowledge (~100 entries already in codebase), then expands with external NinjaTrader marketplace research and classic pattern documentation.
> 
> **Deliverables**:
> - Claude skill (`SKILL.md` + `knowledge.md` index + domain files)
> - 44 DEEP6 signal entries (extracted from existing code)
> - 38 confluence rule entries (extracted from existing RULES.md)
> - ~15 auction theory entries (extracted from existing research)
> - ~12 microstructure theory entries (extracted from existing research)
> - ~15-20 NinjaTrader marketplace/community strategy entries (new research)
> - ~10-15 classic trading pattern entries (new research)
> - Strategy finding methodology guide
> - Academic paper citation index
> - Glossary of trading terminology
> - Registered in CLAUDE.md for automatic discovery
> 
> **Estimated Effort**: Large

## Verification Strategy

> **ZERO HUMAN INTERVENTION** ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ALL verification is agent-executed. No exceptions.

### Test Decision
- **Infrastructure exists**: YES (bun test for dashboard, pytest for Python)
- **Automated tests**: NO ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â this is a content/knowledge project, not code
- **Framework**: N/A
- **Primary verification**: Content accuracy via code cross-reference validation + functional smoke tests

### QA Policy
Every task MUST include agent-executed QA scenarios. Evidence saved to `.sisyphus/evidence/task-{N}-{scenario-slug}.{ext}`.

- **Knowledge entries**: Use Bash (grep/read) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â verify code references exist, entry schema compliance, line counts
- **Skill functionality**: Use Bash ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â verify file structure, CLAUDE.md registration, trigger patterns
- **Smoke tests**: Invoke skill through natural queries, verify response quality

  **What to do**:
  - Document a comprehensive methodology for finding and evaluating NinjaTrader trading strategies:
    - **Where to find strategies**:
      - NinjaTrader User App Share (free community .cs files)
      - NinjaTrader Ecosystem Marketplace (paid vendors)
      - futures.io forum (strategy discussions)
      - NinjaTrader support forum
      - GitHub (open-source NinjaScript)
      - Trading education sites (Jigsaw, Axia Futures, SMB Capital, etc.)
      - Academic papers (quantitative strategies)
    - **How to evaluate a strategy**:
      - Red flags: no verifiable track record, unrealistic claims, no drawdown data, black-box with no logic explanation
      - Green flags: published backtest methodology, realistic Sharpe ratios, clear entry/exit rules, documented risk management
      - Evaluation checklist: logic transparency, market condition awareness, risk management, execution feasibility
    - **Backtesting checklist for NinjaTrader strategies**:
      - Data quality requirements
      - Walk-forward analysis vs simple backtest
      - Slippage and commission assumptions for NQ
      - Survivorship bias awareness
      - Curve fitting detection
    - **Adapting external strategies for DEEP6**:

  Scenario: Edge case ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â query about uncovered topic
    Tool: Bash (read)

  Review all knowledge entries for: accuracy (do code references point to real files/lines?), completeness (ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â°ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¥80% schema fill?), consistency (same schema used across all entries?), clarity (no jargon without definition?). Check for AI slop: excessive cross-references, meta-frameworks, placeholder content, introductory fluff. Verify `wc -l` on all files < 1,500 lines.

## Success Criteria

### Verification Commands
```bash
# Skill structure exists
Test-Path "C:\Users\Tea\DEEP6\.claude\skills\trading-knowledge\SKILL.md"  # Expected: True
Test-Path "C:\Users\Tea\DEEP6\.claude\skills\trading-knowledge\knowledge.md"  # Expected: True

# All domain files present
Get-ChildItem "C:\Users\Tea\DEEP6\.claude\skills\trading-knowledge\domains\*.md" | Measure-Object  # Expected: Count = 7

# All catalog files present
Get-ChildItem "C:\Users\Tea\DEEP6\.claude\skills\trading-knowledge\catalogs\*.md" | Measure-Object  # Expected: Count = 3

# All reference files present
Get-ChildItem "C:\Users\Tea\DEEP6\.claude\skills\trading-knowledge\references\*.md" | Measure-Object  # Expected: Count = 2

# CLAUDE.md registration
Select-String "trading-knowledge" "C:\Users\Tea\DEEP6\CLAUDE.md"  # Expected: match found

# No file exceeds 1500 lines
Get-ChildItem "C:\Users\Tea\DEEP6\.claude\skills\trading-knowledge" -Recurse -Filter "*.md" | ForEach-Object { $lines = (Get-Content $_.FullName | Measure-Object -Line).Lines; if ($lines -gt 1500) { Write-Output "$($_.Name): $lines lines EXCEEDS LIMIT" } }  # Expected: no output
```
